    77                                                   end

    80                                                 task body();
    81                                                   bit [31:0] data;
    82
    83              1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_BAUD, 32'd1, 1);
    84
    85                                                   // Keep loopback enabled while IRQ is disabled. The received byte must stay
    86                                                   // in the FIFO without asserting the interrupt.
    87              1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_CTRL, 32'h3, 4);
    88              1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b0, 1'b1, 4, "reset state with IRQ disabled");
    89              1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_TXDATA, 32'ha5, 2);
    90              1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b0, 1'b0, 360, "RX pending with IRQ disabled");
    91
    92                                                   // Enabling IRQ while data is already pending must assert it immediately.
    93              1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_CTRL, 32'h7, 2);
    94              1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b1, 1'b0, 2, "enable IRQ with pending data");
    95              1                          3         apb_read(ADDR_RXDATA, data, 2);
    96                                                   if (data[7:0] != 8'ha5) begin
    97              1                    ***0***           `uvm_error("IRQ_DATA", $sformatf("read 0x%02h expected 0xa5", data[7:0]))
    98                                                   end
    99              1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b0, 1'b1, 8, "FIFO empty after first read");
    100
    101                                                  // With IRQ enabled, a new RX byte must assert it. Disabling IRQ must lower
    102                                                  // the output without consuming the pending byte.
    103             1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_TXDATA, 32'h3c, 2);
    104             1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b1, 1'b0, 360, "new RX data with IRQ enabled");
    105             1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_CTRL, 32'h3, 2);
    106             1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b0, 1'b0, 2, "disable IRQ with pending data");
    107             1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_CTRL, 32'h7, 2);
    108             1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b1, 1'b0, 2, "re-enable IRQ with pending data");

    20                                               class uart_baud_timing_test extends uart_base_test;
    21              1                    ***0***       `uvm_component_utils(uart_baud_timing_test)

    23                                                 virtual uart_if timing_vif;
    24                                                 bit [31:0] cov_baud_programmed;
    25                                                 bit        cov_timing_pass;
    26                                                 realtime   uart_period;

    28                                                 covergroup baud_timing_cg;
    29                                                   option.per_instance = 1;
    30
    31                                                   cp_baud: coverpoint cov_baud_programmed {
    32                                                     bins zero  = {32'd0};
    33                                                     bins one   = {32'd1};
    34                                                     bins four  = {32'd4};
    35                                                     bins eight = {32'd8};
    36                                                   }
    37
    38                                                   cp_result: coverpoint cov_timing_pass {
    39                                                     bins pass = {1'b1};
    40                                                     illegal_bins fail = {1'b0};
    41                                                   }
    42
    43                                                   cross cp_baud, cp_result;
    44                                                 endgroup

    46                                                 function new(string name = "uart_baud_timing_test", uvm_component parent = null);
    47              1                          3         super.new(name, parent);
    48              1                          3         baud_timing_cg = new();

    51                                                 function void build_phase(uvm_phase phase);

    54              1                    ***0***           `uvm_fatal("NOTIMINGVIF", "UART timing interface is not set")
    55                                                   end
    56                                                 endfunction
    57
    58                                                 task automatic measure_frame(input bit [31:0] programmed_baud);
    59                                                   realtime last_edge;
    60                                                   realtime this_edge;
    61                                                   realtime measured;
    62                                                   realtime expected;
    63                                                   realtime difference;
    64                                                   int unsigned effective_baud;
    65
    66              1                         12         effective_baud = (programmed_baud == 0) ? 1 : programmed_baud;
    67              1                         12         expected = uart_period * effective_baud;
    68              1                         12         cov_timing_pass = 1'b1;
    69
    70                                                   // 0x55 changes level at every frame bit boundary. Starting at the falling
    71                                                   // start-bit edge gives nine independent bit-width measurements.
    72              1                         12         @(negedge timing_vif.tx_o);
    73              1                         12         last_edge = $realtime;
    74              1                         12         for (int i = 0; i < 9; i++) begin
    74              2                        108
    75              1                        108           @(timing_vif.tx_o);
    76              1                        108           this_edge = $realtime;
    77              1                        108           measured = this_edge - last_edge;
    78                                                     difference = (measured > expected) ? (measured - expected)
    79              1                        108                                              : (expected - measured);
    80                                                     if (difference > 1ps) begin
    81              1                    ***0***             cov_timing_pass = 1'b0;
    82                                                       `uvm_error("BAUD_TIMING",
    83                                                                  $sformatf("BAUD=%0d bit=%0d measured=%0t expected=%0t",
    84              1                    ***0***                                  programmed_baud, i, measured, expected))
    85                                                     end
    86              1                        108           last_edge = this_edge;
    87                                                   end
    88
    89              1                         12         cov_baud_programmed = programmed_baud;
    90              1                         12         baud_timing_cg.sample();
    91                                                   `uvm_info("BAUD_TIMING",
    92                                                             $sformatf("BAUD=%0d effective=%0d measured bit width=%0t",
    93                                                                       programmed_baud, effective_baud, expected),
    94              1                         12                   UVM_LOW)
    95                                                 endtask
    96
    97                                                 task automatic run_baud_case(input bit [31:0] programmed_baud);
    98                                                   uart_baud_tx_seq seq;

    101                                                  fork
    102             1                         12           seq.start(env.apb.seqr);
    103             1                         12           measure_frame(programmed_baud);
    104                                                  join
    105                                                endtask
    106
    107                                                task run_phase(uvm_phase phase);
    108                                                  realtime first_edge;
    109
    110             1                          3         phase.raise_objection(this);
    111             1                          3         @(posedge timing_vif.uart_clk);
    112             1                          3         first_edge = $realtime;
    113             1                          3         @(posedge timing_vif.uart_clk);
    114             1                          3         uart_period = $realtime - first_edge;

    116             1                          3         run_baud_case(32'd0);
    117             1                          3         run_baud_case(32'd1);
    118             1                          3         run_baud_case(32'd4);
    119             1                          3         run_baud_case(32'd8);

    26                                                   // Match the DUT's two-stage synchronous reset release in uart_clk.
    27              1                        144         repeat (3) @(posedge vif.uart_clk);
    27              2                        144

    29              1                         48         forever begin

    36                                                 task drive_frame(uart_item tr);
    37                                                   time bit_period;
    38
    39                                                   if (tr.bit_cycles == 0) begin
    40              1                    ***0***           `uvm_fatal("BAD_UART_ITEM", "bit_cycles must be non-zero")
    41                                                   end
    42              1                         90         bit_period = tr.bit_cycles * (2 * cfg.uart_half_ns) * 1ns;
    43
    44                                                   // Re-anchor every frame to the public UART clock before applying the
    45                                                   // independently selected edge offset. This avoids cumulative phase drift
    46                                                   // across long bursts while still keeping the BFM independent of DUT timing pulses.
    47              1                        456         repeat (tr.gap_cycles * tr.bit_cycles) @(posedge vif.uart_clk);
    47              2                        456
    48              1                         90         #(tr.edge_offset_ps * 1ps);

    53              2                        720
    54              1                        720           vif.rx_i <= tr.data[i];
    55              1                        720           #(bit_period);
    56                                                   end
    57
    58              1                         90         vif.rx_i <= tr.frame_err ? 1'b0 : 1'b1;

    63              1                         90         vif.rx_i <= 1'b1;
